INFORMATION ON JNBY GROUP                                                              關於江南布衣

JNBY Design Limited (the “Company” or “JNBY Group”) and its subsidiaries (the          江南布衣有限公司（「本公司」、「公司」或「江南布衣」）及其
“Group” or “we”) are an influential designer brand fashion house based in China.       附屬公司（「本集團」、「集團」或「我們」）是一家位於中國的
We design, promote and sell contemporary apparel, footwear and accessories as          有影響力設計師品牌時尚集團。我們設計、推廣及出售時
well as household products. As of June 30, 2026, our brand portfolio comprises a       尚服裝、鞋類、配飾及家居類產品。截至二零二六年六月
number of brands in three stages — the Mature brand, namely JNBY, three                三十日，我們的品牌組合包括三個階段的多個品牌，即成
Younger brands, namely (i) CROQUIS, (ii) jnby by JNBY and (iii) LESS, as well as       熟品牌JNBY，三個成長品牌(i)速寫、(ii) jnby by JNBY及
various Emerging brands, such as POMME DE TERRE (蓬 馬), JNBYHOME,                       ( i i i ) L E S S ， 以 及 包 括 P O M M E D E T E R R E（ 蓬 馬 ）、
onmygame and B1OCK, each targeting at a distinct customer segment and having a         JNBYHOME、onmygame及B1OCK等 多 個 新 興 品 牌，各 個
uniquely defined design identity based on our Group’s universal brand philosophy —     品牌均各自面向特定細分客戶並擁有基於本集團統一品牌
“Just Naturally Be Yourself”.                                                          理念「自然而然地做自己」(Just Naturally Be Yourself)的獨

                                                                                                        CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT 主席報告




CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告



Dear Shareholders,                                                                    尊敬的各位股東：

On behalf of the board (the “Board”) of directors (the “Directors”) of the Company,   本人謹代表本公司董事（「董事」）會（「董事會」），欣然呈報
I am pleased to present the annual results and business outlook of the Group for      本集團截至二零二六年六月三十日止年度之全年業績及業
the year ended June 30, 2026.                                                         務展望。

ASSESSING THE TIMES AND MARKET DYNAMICS TO                                            審時度勢，深耕品牌生態 — 市場回顧與
DEEPEN BRAND ECOSYSTEM DEVELOPMENT —                                                  策略進展
MARKET REVIEW AND STRATEGIC PROGRESS
In the Fiscal Year 2025/26, driven by continued policies to expand domestic demand    二零二五╱二六財年，中國零售環境在擴內需、促消費政
and boost consumption, China’s retail environment maintained its recovery             策持續發力下延續修復，整體復甦節奏仍然溫和。我們觀
momentum, albeit at a moderate pace. As we have observed, consumer confidence         察到，消費者信心整體趨穩，惟各細分群體消費意願有所

The primary objective of the Group’s dividend policy is to distribute reasonable         本集團股息政策之主要目的為在兼顧集團日常運營、未來

provide returns to Shareholders on their investments, while promoting the                護全體股東的長遠整體利益。
long-term sustainable development of the Group and safeguarding the overall
long-term interests of all Shareholders.

On January 30, 2026, the Company completed the placing of 14,535,000 new                  於二零二六年一月三十日，本公司根據授予董事的一般授
ordinary shares of nominal value of HK$0.01 each (the “Placing Shares”) for cash          權按每股配售股份18.78港元的配售價，完成向不少於六名

pursuant to the general mandate granted to the Directors. The Placing was                 售股份」），以籌集現金。配售事項旨在透過吸引若干高品
undertaken to further broaden the Shareholder base by attracting certain                  質機構投資者進一步擴大股東基礎，進一步加強股份流動
high-quality institutional investors, further enhance the liquidity of the shares and     性，並支持本公司的健康及可持續發展。配售事項所得款
support the healthy and sustainable development of the Company. The Net                   項淨額（經扣除所有適用成本及開支，包括佣金及徵費）約
Proceeds from the Placing, after deducting all applicable costs and expenses,             為270.0百萬港元，相當於每股配售股份的淨價約為18.58
including commission and levies, amounted to approximately HK$270.0 million,              港元，並擬用於(i)加強品牌業務發展；(ii)研發投資以進一
representing a net price of approximately HK$18.58 per Placing Share, and are             步提升綜合市場競爭力；及(iii)一般及企業用途。股份於二
intended to be used for (i) strengthening our brand business development; (ii)            零二六年一月二十二日（即包括配售價在內的發行條款釐
investment in research and development efforts to further enhance our                     定當日）在聯交所所報的收市價為每股股份20.64港元。

NON-COMPETITION UNDERTAKING                                                               不競爭承諾
To safeguard our Group from any potential competition, each of Ms. Li Lin and             為保障本集團免受任何潛在競爭，李琳女士及吳健先生（「契
Mr. Wu Jian (the “Covenantors”) has entered into a deed of non-competition (the           諾承諾人」）各自已於二零一六年十月十三日以本公司為受
“Deed of Non-Competition”) in favour of our Company on October 13, 2016                   益方訂立不競爭契據（「不競爭契據」），據此，契諾承諾人
pursuant to which the Covenantors have unconditionally, irrevocably and jointly and       已無條件、不可撤回地以及共同及各別向本集團承諾，彼
severally undertaken with our Group that they shall not (except through the Group         等不會（透過本集團及透過本集團持有的任何投資或權益
and any investment or interests held through the Group), and shall procure that           則除外）並促使其緊密聯繫人（本集團任何成員公司除外）
his/her close associates (other than any member of our Group) shall not, during           於限制期間（定義見招股章程）不會直接或間接（包括通過

The Group is committed to maintaining a high standard of corporate governance to        本集團致力維持高水準的企業管治，以保障股東權益並提
safeguard the interests of its Shareholders and enhance its value and                   升企業價值及問責性。本公司已採納上市規則附錄C1所載
accountability. The Company has adopted the Corporate Governance Code (the              之企業管治守則（「企業管治守則」）作為其自身的企業管治
“Corporate Governance Code”) contained in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules as its       守則。
own corporate governance code.

Corporate Governance Code during the Fiscal Year 2026. The Company will                 有適用守則條文。本公司將繼續檢討並監察其企業管治常
continue to review and monitor its corporate governance practices in order to           規，以確保遵守企業管治守則。
ensure compliance with the Corporate Governance Code.

their consistency with the corporate culture of the Company. All Directors act with     的企業文化一致。所有董事行事持正不阿、以身作則，致

Corporate Strategies                                                                    公司戰略

•     Adhere to design-driven and realise brand strength-driven                         •   堅持設計驅動，實現品牌力驅動

The Board is responsible for the overall leadership of the Group, oversees the       董事會負責本集團的整體領導，並監察本集團的策略性決
Group’s strategic decisions and monitors business and performance. The Board has     定以及監察業務及表現。董事會已向本集團的高級管理層
delegated the authority and responsibility for day-to-day management and             授予本集團日常管理及營運的權力及責任。為監察本公司
operation of the Group to the senior management of the Group. To oversee             事務的特定範疇，董事會已成立三個董事會委員會，包括

Committees”). The Board has delegated to the Board Committees responsibilities
as set out in their respective terms of reference.

BOARD PERFORMANCE ASSESSMENT                                                            董事會表現評估
During the Fiscal Year 2026, an independent third-party consulting agency was           於二零二六財年，獨立第三方企業諮詢機構根據二零二六
engaged under a service agreement entered into in June 2026 to conduct an               年六月訂立的服務協議，獲委任開展外部董事會表現評估。
external Board performance assessment. The assessment report was dated August           評估報告日期為二零二六年八月二十一日。審查聚焦於董
21, 2026. The review focused on the Board as a whole, including the Board’s             事會整體，涵蓋董事會整體表現、技能、專業知識與資格、
overall performance, skills, expertise and qualifications, the operation of the Board   董事會委員會的運作情況，以及董事的集體貢獻、參與度、
Committees, and the collective contribution, participation, independent views and       獨立意見及培訓情況。
training of Directors.

Assessment Focus                                                                        評估重點

The assessment covered five dimensions: (i) Governance Structure Effectiveness; (ii)    是次評估涵蓋五個範疇：(i)管治架構成效；(ii)策略監督職
Strategic Stewardship; (iii) Risk and Internal Control Oversight; (iv) Board Dynamics   能；(iii)風險與內部控制監督；(iv)董事會運作動態與行為；
and Behavior; and (v) Stakeholder and Sustainability Oversight.                         及(v)持份者與可持續發展監察。

Board Evaluation Process                                                                董事會評估流程

The methodology of consulting agency comprised a review of governance documents,        諮詢機構的方法涵蓋管治文件審閱、重點一對一訪談、管
focused one-to-one interviews, analysis of governance information, peer and             治資料分析、同業及領先實踐對標，以及結果溝通與行動
leading-practice benchmarking, and communication of the results and action              計劃制定。
planning.

Assessment Findings                                                                     評估發現

The Board was of the view that the Board and each of its committees of the              董事會認為本公司董事會及各委員會有效運作。「持份者與
Company were operated effectively. “Stakeholder and Sustainability Oversight” and       可持續發展監察」以及「策略監督職能」是相對優勢，而「董
“Strategic Stewardship” were relative strengths, while “Board Dynamics and              事會運作動態與行為」以及「管治架構成效」則被確定為需
Behavior” and “Governance Structure Effectiveness” were identified as the principal     要改進的主要領域。
areas for improvement.

•     The Board had a sound diversity and independence foundation, with                 •   董事會具備良好的多元化與獨立性基礎，獨立非執
      independent non-executive Directors representing more than one-third of the           行董事佔董事會成員三分之一以上，女性董事佔比
      Board and female Directors representing 37.5%.                                        達37.5%。

•     External perspectives were brought into strategic discussions, while investor     •   戰略討論中引入外部視角，同時通過多種渠道收集
      feedback was collected through multiple channels and supported by a clear             投資者回饋，並以清晰透明的股息政策作為支撐。
      and transparent dividend policy.

      provided a sound basis for risk and internal control supervision; Board               與內部控制監管奠定堅實基礎；董事會會議出席率

      approval procedures.

arrangements.

The Company recognises the importance of the diversity of members of the Board         本公司認同董事會成員多元化對企業管治及董事會行之有
to the effectiveness of corporate governance and the Board. In order to enhance        效的重要性，提名委員會已制定董事會多元化政策，以確

experience and perspectives of the members of the Board. Details are set out

the principle of meritocracy based on the demand of daily business and                 常的業務需求為基準，並考慮董事會成員多元化的裨益。
consideration of benefits due to diversity of Board members. The principal             提名委員會主要負責物色具備合適資格可擔任董事的人士

ensuring equal opportunities for male and female employees. The Group regards          集團將性別平等作為多元包容文化的重要組成部分，落實

The Group identifies “Diversity and Inclusion” as its core values. Committed to        集團將「多元包容」列為核心價值觀，致力於建設客觀公正

Nevertheless, the Group has not put in place any externally binding quantitative       則是以能力為本、兼顧性別多元，而非以性別配額作為遴

The overarching principle of its diversity policy is merit-based appointment

MANAGEMENT

The Company has adopted a remuneration policy for the Directors and senior            本公司已採納一項董事及高級管理人員薪酬政策，總體原
management with the overall principle of motivating and promoting enthusiasm,         則為達公司戰略目標而激勵及推動各級員工工作熱情、積
initiative and spirit of the masters of employees at all levels to achieve the        極性及主人翁精神；
Company’s strategic objectives.

The purpose of the Company’s remuneration policy is to provide market competitive     公司薪酬政策目的是公司在合法合規的前提下，提供具有
compensation, attract and retain outstanding talents, and build a high-performance    市場競爭力的薪酬，吸引和保留優秀人才，組建高績效、
and high-quality team on the premise of legal compliance, so as to motivate           高素質團隊，從而激發員工效能與團隊生產力。
employee effectiveness and team productivity.

•     The remuneration strategy focuses on the principles of legal compliance,        •   薪酬策略著重合法合規性、市場競爭性、員工激勵
      market competitiveness, employee incentive, internal equity and cost                性、內部公平性和成本節約性原則；
      savings;

•     Offer market-leading remuneration for core functions and key positions that     •   對支持公司戰略實現的核心職能和關鍵崗位，提供
      support the Company to realise its strategies;                                      領先市場的薪酬水平；

•     The Company will continue to pay attention to the fairness of remuneration      •   公司持續關注薪酬給付的公平性，在滿足公司需求
      payment, and carry out reasonable remuneration cost control on the                  的前提下進行合理的薪酬成本控制；及
      premise of meeting the needs of the Company; and

•     For management purpose, the remuneration is adjusted annually based on          •   薪酬管理遵循以年度為調整頻率；以績效結果為薪
      performance following the process of reporting — review — verification —            酬調整依據；以報 — 審 — 核 — 准為薪酬調整流
      approval.                                                                           程。

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                                   風險管理及內部監控

The Group believes that good corporate governance is very important for                本集團相信良好的企業管治對於維持及促進投資者信心及
maintaining and promoting investor confidence and for the sustainable development      本集團的可持續發展非常重要，穩健的風險管理和內部監
of the Group, and a robust risk management and internal control framework is an        控框架是實現策略目標和維持業務持續增長的重要組成部
integral part of achieving strategic objectives and maintaining sustainable business   分。因此，本集團一直致力於提升企業管治水平，建立高
growth. The Group has therefore made continuous efforts to uplift its quality of       效的風險管理（包括ESG風險）系統及內部監控，採取一系
corporate governance. It has established effective risk management (including ESG      列措施保證該等系統的健全性及有效性，從而得以確保本
risks) and internal control systems, and adopted a series of measures to ensure        集團資產安全及維護股東利益。
their soundness and effectiveness. As a result, the Group is able to safeguard its
assets and protect the interests of its Shareholders.

1.    GENERAL INFORMATION                                                                   1.      一般資料

JNBY Design Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated in the Cayman Islands on               江南布衣有限公司（「本公司」）於二零一二年十一月二十六
26 November 2012 as an exempted company with limited liability under the                    日 根 據 開 曼 群 島 法 例 第22章 公 司 法（一 九 六 一 年 第3號 法
Companies Act, Cap. 22 (Law 3 of 1961, as consolidated and revised) of the Cayman           律，經綜合及修訂）在開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限責任
Islands. The address of the Company’s registered office is Cricket Square, Hutchins         公司。本公司的註冊辦事處位於Cricket Square, Hutchins
Drive, P.O. Box 2681, Grand Cayman KY1-1111, Cayman Islands. Pursuant to the                Drive, P.O. Box 2681, Grand Cayman KY1-1111, Cayman
resolution passed by the board of directors of the Company (the “Board”) on 8 June          Islands。根據本公司董事會（「董事會」）於二零一六年六月
2016, the Company changed its name from Croquis Investment Limited to the                   八日通過的決議案，本公司的名稱由Croquis Investment
present one.                                                                                Limited更改為現時名稱。

The Company and its subsidiaries (collectively, the “Group”) are primarily engaged          本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主要於中華人民共和
in the design, marketing and sales of fashion apparel, accessory products and               國（「中國」）及海外從事時尚服裝、配飾產品及家居用品的
household goods in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”) and overseas.                 設計、營銷及銷售。

Kong Limited on 31 October 2016.                                                            限公司主板上市。

3.      FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                       3.      財務風險管理
3.1     Financial risk factors                                                          3.1     財務風險因素

The Group’s businesses expose it to a variety of financial risks: market risk           本集團的業務面臨多項財務風險：市場風險（包括外匯風
(including foreign exchange risk, cash flow and fair value interest rate risk and       險、現金流量及公允價值利率風險及價格風險）、信貸風險
price risk), credit risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk management        及流動性風險。本集團的整體風險管理計劃專注於金融市
programme focuses on the unpredictability of financial markets and seeks to             場的不可預測性，並尋求盡量降低對本集團財務表現可能
minimise potential adverse effects on the Group’s financial performance. The Group      構成的不利影響。本集團目前並無使用任何衍生金融工具
currently does not use any derivative financial instruments to hedge certain risk       以對沖所承受的若干風險。
exposures.

(a)     Market risk                                                                     (a)     市場風險

(i)     Foreign exchange risk                                                           (i)     外匯風險

Foreign exchange risk arises when future commercial transactions or recognised          當未來商業交易或集團實體的已確認資產或負債以非該實
assets or liabilities of a Group entity are denominated in a currency that is not the   體的功能貨幣計值時，則產生外匯風險。本集團通過定期
entity’s functional currency. The Group manages its foreign exchange risk by            審閱本集團淨外匯風險來管理其外匯風險。
performing regular reviews of the Group’s net foreign exchange exposures.

The Group operates in the PRC with most of the Group’s transactions denominated         本集團在中國經營，本集團大部分交易以人民幣計值及結
and settled in RMB. The Group’s assets and liabilities, and transactions arising        算。由於本集團於二零二六年及二零二五年六月三十日的
from its operations do not expose the Group to material foreign exchange risk as        綜合財務狀況表中大部分已確認資產及負債以本集團公司
the Group’s most recognised assets and liabilities in the consolidated statement of     各自功能貨幣計值，故本集團的資產及負債以及其經營活
financial position as at 30 June 2026 and 2025 are denominated in the respective        動中產生的交易不會使本集團面臨重大外匯風險。
Group companies’ functional currencies.

(ii)    Cash flow and fair value interest rate risk                                     (ii)    現金流量及公允價值利率風險

The Group’s income and operating cash flows are substantially independent of            本集團的收入及經營現金流量基本上獨立於市場利率的變
changes in market interest rates. The Group has no significant interest-bearing         動。除初始期限超過三個月的定期存款（附註21）、現金及
assets and liabilities, except for the term deposits with initial term over 3 months    現金等價物（附註22）及借款（附註25）外，本集團並無重大
(Note 21), cash and cash equivalents (Note 22) and borrowings (Note 25). Those          計息資產及負債。以浮動利率持有的資產及負債使本集團
carried at floating rates expose the Group to cash flow interest rate risk whereas      面臨現金流量利率風險，而以固定利率持有的資產及負債
those carried at fixed rates expose the Group to fair value interest rate risk.         使本集團面臨公允價值利率風險。

Bank balances are excluded from sensitivity analysis as the management considers        由於管理層認為浮動利率銀行結餘產生的現金流量利率風
that the exposure of cash flow interest rate risk arising from variable-rate bank       險並不重大，因此敏感度分析並不包括銀行結餘。
balances is insignificant.

Loan carried at fixed rates expose the Group to fair value interest rate risk.          以固定利率計息的貸款使本集團面臨公允價值利率風險。
Management does not anticipate significant impact to the interest-bearing loan          管理層預期，公允價值利率風險不會對計息貸款產生重大
resulted from fair value interest rate risk, because the interest rates are not         影響，原因是利率預期不會出現重大變動。
expected to change significantly.

(iii)   Price risk                                                                      (iii)   價格風險

The Group’s exposure to price risk arises from investments held by the Group and        本集團面臨的價格風險來自於本集團所持有分類為按公允
classified as financial assets at FVTPL. To manage its price risk arising from the      價值計入損益的金融資產的投資。本集團多元化其投資組
investments, the Group diversifies its portfolio. The investments made by the Group     合以管理因投資而產生的價格風險。本集團的投資旨在提
are either for the purpose of improving investment yield or for strategic purpose.      高投資收益或作戰略發展用途。

3.     FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT (CONTINUED)                                              3.    財務風險管理（續）
3.1    Financial risk factors (Continued)                                                 3.1   財務風險因素（續）

(b)   Credit risk                                                                         (b)   信貸風險

Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties default on their           信貸風險指本集團之交易對手未能履行其合約義務並對本
contractual obligations resulting in financial losses to the Group. Credit risk is        集團造成財務虧損之風險。信貸風險按組別基準管理。信
managed on a group basis. Credit risk arises from cash and cash equivalents, term         貸風險來自現金及現金等價物、初始期限超過三個月的定
deposits with initial term over 3 months, amounts due from related parties, trade         期存款、應收關聯方款項、應收賬款及其他應收款項。信
receivables and other receivables. The maximum exposure to credit risk is                 貸風險的最大敞口由綜合財務狀況表中每項金融資產的賬
represented by the carrying amount of each financial asset in the consolidated            面值表示。
statement of financial position.

(i)   Risk management                                                                     (i)   風險管理

Credit risk on cash and cash equivalents, and term deposits with initial term over 3      現金及現金等價物以及初始期限超過三個月的定期存款的
months is limited because the counterparties are reputable banks with high credit         信貸風險有限，因為交易對手均為信譽良好且獲信用評級
ratings assigned by credit agencies. The Group assessed 12m ECL for cash and              機構授予高信用評級的銀行。本集團參照外部信用評級機
cash equivalents, and term deposits with initial term over 3 months by reference to       構所公佈的相關信用評級等級的違約概率及違約損失率的
information relating to probability of default and loss given default of the respective   相關資料，評估現金及現金等價物以及初始期限超過三個
credit rating grades published by external credit rating agencies.                        月的定期存款的12個月預期信貸虧損。

taken to recover overdue debts. The Group does not have significant credit risk           一組交易對手的重大信貸風險。由於客戶群龐大且互不相

which it sells its goods and services to be the most relevant factors, and              應調整過往虧損率。
accordingly adjusts the historical loss rates based on expected changes in these
factors.

The table below analyses financial instruments carried at fair value, by valuation               下表利用估值法分析按公允價值列賬的金融工具。不同層
method. The different levels have been defined as follows:                                       級界定如下：

•        Level 1 fair value measurements are those derived from quoted prices                    •       第一級公允價值計量乃源自相同資產或負債於活躍
         (unadjusted) in active markets for identical assets or liabilities.                             市場的報價（未經調整）。

•        Level 2 fair value measurements are those derived from inputs other than                •       第二級公允價值計量乃源自除第一級所包括的報價
         quoted prices included within Level 1 that are observable for the asset or                      外，資產或負債的直接（如價格）或間接（即價格衍
         liability, either directly (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived from prices).           生物）可觀察的輸入數據。

•        Level 3 fair value measurements are those derived from valuation techniques             •       第三級公允價值計量乃源自估值技術，且對公允價
         for which the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value                          值計量具有重大影響的最低等級輸入數據不可觀察
         measurement is unobservable (significant unobservable input).                                   （重大不可觀察輸入數據）。

Specific valuation techniques used to value financial instruments include:                       用於金融工具估值之特定估值技術包括：

•        Quoted market prices or dealer quotes for similar instruments.                          •       同類工具之市場報價或交易商報價。

•        Other techniques, such as discounted cash flow analysis, are used to                    •       其他技術（例如貼現現金流量分析）用於釐定剩餘金
         determine fair value for the remaining financial instruments.                                   融工具的公允價值。

4.      CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND KEY                                          4.      重大會計判斷及估計不確定性主要
        SOURCES OF ESTIMATE UNCERTAINTY                                                         來源
In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, the directors of the Company     於應用本集團的會計政策時，本公司董事須就並未在其他
are required to make judgements, estimates and assumptions about the carrying           來源顯示的資產及負債的賬面值作出判斷、估計及假設。
amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources.     估計及相關假設乃基於過往經驗及其他相關因素而作出。
The estimates and underlying assumptions are based on historical experience and         實際結果可能有別於該等估計。
other factors that are considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from
these estimates.

The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an on-going basis.             估計及相關假設乃按持續經營基準審閱。倘會計估計的修
Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in the period in which the             訂僅影響變動期間，則有關修訂會於該期間確認；或倘會
estimate is revised if the revision affects only that period, or in the period of the   計估計的修訂對當期及未來期間均有影響，則有關修訂會
revision and future periods if the revision affects both current and future periods.    於修訂期間及未來期間確認。

(a)   Net realisable value of inventories                                               (a)   存貨之可變現淨值

Net realisable value of inventories is the estimated selling price in the ordinary      存貨之可變現淨值乃於日常業務中之估計售價，扣除銷售
course of business, less estimated costs necessary to make the sale.                    所需估計成本。

The Group assesses the net realisable value of the inventories as well as the           本集團評估各報告期末之存貨可變現淨值以及所需計提的
required amount of impairment of inventory provision at the end of each reporting       存貨減值撥備金額，此評估涉及基於考慮庫齡結構、未來
period, which involves significant judgement on determination of the estimated net      銷售預測、預估未來售價以及銷售相關存貨所需估計成本
realisable value of the inventories based on the consideration of key factors such      等關鍵因素就釐定存貨之估計可變現淨值作出的重大判斷。
as ageing profile, future sales projection, estimated future selling prices and         本集團亦對存貨的毀損狀況進行定期檢查並於各報告期末
estimated costs necessary to make the sale of the respective inventories. The           就該等受損存貨評估是否需作出撇減。
Group performs regular check on the physical conditions of inventories and
assesses possible write-down for any damaged inventories at the end of each
reporting period.

These key estimates are based on the current market condition and the historical        該等主要估計乃根據目前市況以及銷售類似類型產品的過
experience of selling products of similar type, which are reassessed at the end of      往經驗作出，並於各報告期末進行重估，原因是其可能因
each reporting period as they could change significantly as a result of changes in      為客戶喜好轉變及競爭對手因應嚴峻行業週期而採取的行
customer taste and competitors actions in response to severe industry cycle.            動而出現重大變動。

(b)   Right of return                                                                   (b)   退貨權

The Group offers right of return to distributors and end customers. The Group           本集團向經銷商及終端客戶提供退貨權。本集團管理層估
management estimates the amount of returns associated with sales in a specific          計與特定期間銷售相關的退貨金額，並從該等銷售產生的
period, which are deducted from the total revenue arising from such sales.              總收入中扣除。管理層基於歷史經驗使用預期價值法估計
Historical experience is used to estimate and provide for the returns, using the        退貨及就退貨作出撥備，且收益僅於極可能不會出現大量
expected value method, and revenue is only recognised to the extent that it is          退貨的情況下，方獲確認。管理層相信本集團已具備充足
highly probable that a significant reversal will not occur. Management believes that    過往經驗及模式，根據不同客戶情況，如經銷商、線下零
the Group has possessed sufficient historical experience and patterns to estimate       售客戶、線上零售客戶等估計退貨。有關估計根據競爭格
sales returns based on different customer profiles, e.g. distributors, offline retail   局、經濟環境及客戶喜好轉變週期性進行。
customers, online retail customers, etc. Such estimates are performed on a
periodic basis taking into account the competitive landscape, economic
environment, and changes in customer taste.

(c)   Membership-based customer loyalty program                                          (c)   會員制客戶忠誠度計劃

The Group offers a membership-based customer loyalty program, under which                本集團提供會員制客戶忠誠度計劃，據此加入會員的客戶
customers who joined the membership are able to accumulate reward points                 可通過購買商品積累獎勵積分並能將該等獎勵積分兌換為
through purchases of goods and could redeem these reward points for vouchers             後續購買中可享受折扣的抵用券。本集團基於預期兌換積
entitling discount on a subsequent purchase. The Group accrues for contract              分的估計獨立售價隨著會員積累積分產生合約負債。會員
liability as members accumulate points based on the estimated standalone selling         兌換獎勵時，應計合約負債相應減少。
price of the points expected to be redeemed. When members redeem awards, the
accrued contract liability is reduced correspondingly.

(a)   Determination of the lease term                                                    (a)   釐定租期

In determining the lease term, management considers all facts and circumstances          在釐定租期時，管理層考慮促使行使延期權或不行使終止
that create an economic incentive to exercise an extension option, or not exercise a     權之經濟誘因的所有事實及情況。僅在能合理地確定租賃
termination option. Extension options (or periods after termination options) are only    可延期（或不予終止）的情況下，延期權（或終止權後之期
included in the lease term if the lease is reasonably certain to be extended (or not     間）才會計入租期。
terminated).

The following factors are normally the most relevant:                                    下列因素一般最具關連性：

•      If there are significant penalties to terminate (or not extend), the Group is     •     倘終止（或不延期）涉及巨額罰款，本集團通常會合
       typically reasonably certain to extend (or not terminate);                              理確定延期（或不終止）；

•      If any leasehold improvements are expected to have a significant remaining        •     倘預期任何租賃改善裝修具有重大剩餘價值，本集
       value, the Group is typically reasonably certain to extend (or not terminate);          團通常會合理確定延期（或不終止）；

•      Otherwise, the Group considers other factors including historical lease           •     否則，本集團考慮其他因素（包括歷史租期及取代已
       durations and the costs and business disruption required to replace the                 租賃資產所需成本及涉及的業務中斷）。
       leased asset.

The lease term is reassessed if any option is actually exercised (or not exercised)      倘實際行使（或不行使）或本集團有責任行使（或不行使）選
or the Group becomes obliged to exercise (or not exercise) it. The assessment of         擇權，則對租賃期進行重新評估。僅於發生重大事件或情
reasonable certainty is only revised if a significant event or a significant change in   況發生重大變化而影響評估，且在承租人的控制範圍內，
circumstances occurs, which affects this assessment, and that is within the control      方會對評估合理確定性進行修訂。
of the lessee.

5.    REVENUE AND SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                5.    收入及分部資料
(a)   Revenue                                                                        (a)   收入

The Group is primarily engaged in the design, marketing and sales of fashion         本集團主要從事時尚服裝、配飾產品及家居用品的設計、
apparel, accessory products and household goods. Revenue from sales of goods is      營銷及銷售。銷售貨品的收入於產品的控制權轉讓予客戶
recognised at the point in time when control of the products is transferred to the   時確認。
customers.

(b)   Segment information                                                            (b)   分部資料

The Group operates multiple operating segments based on different brands. The        本集團基於不同品牌經營多個經營分部。經營分部的報告
operating segments are reported in a manner consistent with the internal reporting   方式與提供予主要營運決策者（「主要營運決策者」）
                                                                                                            （即執行
provided to the chief operating decision-maker (the “CODM”), the executive           董事）的內部報告貫徹一致。
directors.

Management has determined the operating segments based on the information            管理層已根據主要營運決策者審閱的資料釐定經營分部，

(g)    Accounting policy of revenue recognition                                            (g)   收入確認的會計政策

The Group is primarily engaged in the design, marketing and sales of fashion               本集團主要從事時尚服裝、配飾產品及家居用品的設計、
apparel, accessory products and household goods. Revenue from sales of goods is            營銷及銷售。銷售貨品的收入於產品的控制權轉讓予客戶
recognised at the point in time when control of the products is transferred to the         時確認。交付產品前自經銷商收取的墊付款項確認為合約
customers. Receipt in advance from distributors before delivery of products are            負債。
recognised as contract liabilities.

In determining the transaction price for the sale of goods, the Group considers the        於釐定銷售貨品的交易價時，本集團考慮到可變代價的影
effect of variable considerations, and consideration payable to the customers. No          響及應付客戶的代價。概無重大財務部分被視為呈列為包
significant financing component is deemed present as the sales are made with a             含與市場慣例一致的信貸條款的銷售。
credit term consistent with market practice.

(i)   Sales of goods — distributors                                                        (i)   銷售貨品 — 經銷商

A significant portion of the Group’s products are sold to distributors, who have           本集團相當部分的產品售予經銷商，經銷商可酌情決定於
discretion over both the selling price and the distribution channels for such              其指定地理區域銷售該等產品之售價及經銷方式。經銷商
products to be sold in their designated geographical areas. Distributors are               一般須於作出購買訂單時支付按金，並須於產品交付前悉
generally required to pay deposits when placing purchase orders and are required           數結算。
to settle the full payment prior to delivery of the products.

Revenues are recognised upon delivery, which occurs when distributors pick up              收入於交付（即經銷商於本集團不動產提取貨品或貨品交
goods at the Group’s premises or when goods are handed over to a third party               付經銷商指定之第三方代理）時確認。交付乃於廢棄及虧損
forwarder as designated by a specific distributor. Delivery occurs when the risks of       風險已轉移至經銷商（即貨品根據銷售合約交付予經銷商
obsolescence and loss are transferred to the distributors, i.e., when the goods are        或第三方代理，或接收條款已失效，或本集團擁有客觀證
delivered to the distributors or the third party forwarders in accordance with the         據證明已滿足所有接收標準且概無可影響經銷商接受產品
sales contract, or the acceptance provisions have lapsed or the Group has objective        之未履行責任）時達成。
evidence that all criteria for acceptance have been satisfied and there is no
unfulfilled obligation that could affect the distributors’ acceptance of the products.

The Group offers volume rebates to distributors as agreed in the sales contracts.          本集團就銷售合約協定向經銷商提供批量返利。該等銷售
Revenue from these sales is recognised based on the price specified in the                 的收入乃基於合約規定的價格，經扣除估計批量返利後確
contract, net of the estimated volume rebates. Historical experience is used to            認。本公司利用歷史經驗採用預計估值法估計及提供返利，
estimate and provide for the rebates, using the expected value method, and                 且收入僅於重大撥回極大可能不會產生時確認。直至報告
revenue is only recognised to the extent that it is highly probable that a significant     期末，當預期向經銷商應付有關銷售的批量返利時確認退
reversal will not occur. A refund liability (included in accruals and other liabilities)   款負債（計入應計費用及其他負債）。
is recognised for expected volume rebates payable to distributors in relation to
sales made until the end of the reporting period.

(c)   The Group’s leasing activities                                                 (c)   本集團的租賃活動

The Group leases various offices and retail stores. Rental contracts are typically   本集團租賃多個辦公室及零售店。租賃合約通常有三個月
made for fixed periods of 3 months to 6 years but may have extension options as      至六年的固定期限，惟可能具有下文所述之延期權。租賃
described below. Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain a     條款按個別基準磋商，包含各種不同條款及條件。租賃協
wide range of different terms and conditions. The lease agreements do not impose     議並無施加任何契諾，惟出租人持有的租賃資產中的抵押
any covenants other than the security interests in the leased assets that are held   權益除外。租賃資產不得用作借款之抵押品。
by the lessor. Leased assets may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.

(d)   Variable lease payments                                                        (d)   可變租賃付款

from a store. Variable payment terms are used for certain reasons, including         用可變付款條款有若干原因，包括降低新開商舖的固定成
minimising the fixed costs base for newly established stores. Variable lease         本基礎。按銷售額計算的可變租賃付款於符合產生付款的
payments that depend on sales are recognised in profit or loss in the period in      條件之期間的損益內確認入賬。
which the condition that triggers those payments occurs.

(f)   Accounting policy of leases                                                       (f)   租賃會計政策

Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a corresponding liability at the      租賃乃在租賃資產可供本集團使用當日確認為使用權資產
date at which the leased asset is available for use by the Group.                       及相應負債。

Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components. The Group elects not         合約可能包含租賃及非租賃組成部分。本集團選擇將租賃
to separate lease and non-lease components and accounts for these as a single           及非租賃組成部分入賬為單一租賃組成部分，並無將兩者
lease component.                                                                        區分。

Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured on a present value   租賃產生之資產及負債初步以現值進行計量。租賃負債包
basis. Lease liabilities include the net present value of the following lease           括以下租賃付款之淨現值：
payments:

•     fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments), less any lease            •     固定付款（包括實質固定付款）減任何應收租賃優惠
      incentives receivable

•     amounts expected to be payable by the Group under residual value                  •     剩餘價值擔保下之本集團預期應付款項，及
      guarantees, and

•     payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the lease term reflects the   •     終止租賃所支付之罰款（倘租賃期反映本集團行使
      Group exercising that option.                                                           該選擇權）。

Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension options are also           計量負債時亦包括根據合理確定延期權作出之租賃付款。
included in the measurement of the liability.

The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in the lease. If     租賃付款採用租賃所隱含之利率予以貼現。倘無法釐定該
that rate cannot be readily determined, which is generally the case for leases in the   利率（本集團之租賃一般屬此類情況），則使用承租人之增
Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate that the         量借款利率，即個別承租人在類似經濟環境中以類似條款、
individual lessee would have to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an          抵押及條件借入獲得類似使用權資產價值之資產所需資金
asset of similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment      所必須支付之利率。
with similar terms, security and conditions.

(f)    Accounting policy of leases (Continued)                                            (f)   租賃會計政策（續）

To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                                   為釐定增量借款利率，本集團：

•      where possible, uses recent third-party financing received by the individual       •     在可能情況下，使用個別承租人最近獲得之第三方
       lessee as a starting point, adjusted to reflect changes in financing conditions          融資為出發點作出調整以反映自獲得第三方融資以
       since third party financing was received                                                 來融資條件之變動

•      uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free interest rate adjusted       •     使用累加法，首先就本集團所持有租賃之信貸風險
       for credit risk for leases held by the Group, which does not have recent third           （最近並無第三方融資）調整無風險利率，及
       party financing, and

•      makes adjustments specific to the lease, e.g. term, country, currency and          •     針對租賃進行調整，例如期限、國家、貨幣及抵押。
       security.

Lease payments are allocated between principal and finance cost. The finance cost         租賃付款於本金及融資成本之間作出分配。融資成本在租
is charged to profit or loss over the lease period so as to produce a constant            賃期間於損益扣除，藉以令各期間的負債餘額的期間利率
periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance of the liability for each period.      一致。

Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the following:                        使用權資產按成本計量，包括以下各項：

•      the amount of the initial measurement of lease liability;                          •     租賃負債之初步計量金額；

•      any lease payments made at or before the commencement date less any lease          •     開始日期或之前所作之任何租賃付款，減去所得之
       incentives received;                                                                     任何租賃優惠；

•      any initial direct costs.                                                          •     任何初始直接成本。

Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the shorter of the asset’s useful      使用權資產一般於資產可使用年期或租賃期（以較短者為
life and the lease term on a straight-line basis. If the Group is reasonably certain      準）按直線法計算折舊。倘本集團合理確定行使延期權，則
to exercise an extension option, the right-of-use asset is depreciated over the           使用權資產於相關資產之可使用年期內予以折舊。
underlying asset’s useful life.

A change in the consideration for the lease, without increasing or decreasing the         在不擴大或縮小租賃範圍的情況下，租賃代價的變動導致
scope of the lease, results in a remeasurement of the lease liability and a               對租賃負債的重新計量並對使用權資產作出相應調整。本
corresponding adjustment to the right-of-use asset. The Group remeasures the              集團使用租賃隱含的利率重新計量剩餘租期的租賃負債，
lease liability, using the interest rate implicit in the lease for the remainder of the   並對使用權資產作出相應調整。倘租賃隱含的利率無法可
lease term, and it makes a corresponding adjustment to the right-of-use asset. The        靠釐定，則本集團使用其修改生效日期的增量借款利率。
Group uses its incremental borrowing rate at the effective date of modification if
the interest rate implicit in the lease is not readily determinable.

Payments associated with short-term leases of stores and offices and all leases of        與商舖及辦公室短期租賃及所有租賃低價值資產相關之付
low-value assets are recognised on a straight-line basis as an expense in profit or       款以直線法於損益中確認為開支。短期租賃指租期為12個
loss. Short-term leases are leases with a lease term of 12 months or less. Low-           月或少於12個月的租賃。低價值資產包括資訊科技設備及

(a)   Accounting policy of intangible assets                                           (a)   無形資產會計政策

Computer software and trademarks                                                       電腦軟件及商標

Acquired computer software programs and trademarks are shown at historical cost        所購電腦軟件程序及商標以歷史成本減累計攤銷及累計減
less accumulated amortisation and accumulated impairment if any. Acquired              值（如有）列賬。所購電腦軟件程序乃基於購入及使用特定
computer software programs are capitalised on the basis of the costs incurred to       軟件所產生的成本資本化。該等成本在估計可使用年期10
acquire and bring to use the specific software. These costs are amortised over         年內予以攤銷。
their estimated useful lives of 10 years.

Brands                                                                                 品牌

The brands were acquired as part of a business combination. They are recognised        該等品牌乃作為業務合併的一部分被收購。彼等於收購日
at their fair value at the date of acquisition and are subsequently amortised on a     期按彼等之公允價值確認，其後根據彼等之估計可使用年
straight-line based on their estimated useful lives of 10 years.                       期十年以直線法攤銷。

Goodwill                                                                               商譽

Goodwill is measured as the excess of the sum of the consideration transferred,        商譽按轉讓代價、於被收購方的任何非控股權益金額及收
the amount of any non-controlling interests in the acquiree, and the fair value of     購方先前持有被收購方股權（如有）的公允價值之和，與於
the acquirer’s previously held equity interest in the acquiree (if any) over the net   收購日期所收購可識別資產及所承擔負債淨值的差額計量。
amount of the identifiable assets acquired and the liabilities assumed as at           收購附屬公司產生的商譽計入無形資產。
acquisition date. Goodwill on acquisitions of subsidiaries is included in intangible
assets.

Goodwill is allocated to cash-generating units (“CGUs”) for the purpose of             商譽被分配至現金產生單位（「現金產生單位」）以進行減值
impairment testing. The allocation is made to those CGUs or group of CGUs that         測試。該分配乃分配至預期將自產生商譽之業務合併受益
are expected to benefit from the business combination in which the goodwill arose.     之現金產生單位或現金產生單位組別。各單位或單位組別
The units or groups of units are identified at the lowest level at which goodwill is   為 就 內 部 管 理 目 的 而 監 察 商 譽 之 最 低 層 次。人 民 幣
monitored for internal management purposes. The goodwill of RMB51,352,000 is           51,352,000元之商譽被分配至慧聚並由管理層按慧聚層面
allocated to Huiju and monitored by management at Huiju level.                         監察。

Goodwill is not amortised but it is tested for impairment annually, or more            商譽不會攤銷，但每年作減值測試，倘有事件或情況變化
frequently if events or changes in circumstances indicate that it might be impaired.   顯示商譽可能減值，則會更頻密地進行測試。包含商譽之
The carrying value of the CGUs or group of CGUs containing the goodwill is             現金產生單位或現金產生單位組別之賬面值會與可收回數
compared to the recoverable amount, which is the higher of value in use and the        額（為 使 用 價 值 與 公 允 價 值 減 出 售 成 本 之 較 高 者）進 行 比
fair value less costs of disposal. Any impairment is recognised immediately as an      較。任何減值須即時確認為開支且不得在之後期間撥回。
expense and is not subsequently reversed. Gains and losses on the disposal of          出售現金產生單位或現金產生單位組別產生之利得及虧損
CGUs or group of CGUs include the carrying amount of goodwill relating to the          包括與已出售現金產生單位或現金產生單位組別有關之商
CGUs or group of CGUs disposed of.                                                     譽之賬面值。

(a)   Accounting policy of inventories                                                 (a)   存貨會計政策

Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable value. Cost is          存貨按成本及可變現淨值兩者中的較低者列賬。成本以加
determined using the weighted average method. The cost of finished goods               權平均法釐定。製成品的成本包括原材料及（倘適用）將存
comprises raw materials and, where applicable, sub-contracting costs that have         貨達到現況所產生的分包費用。可變現淨值為在日常業務
been incurred in bringing the inventories to their present condition. Net realisable   過程中的估計售價，減銷售相關存貨所需估計成本。進行
value is the estimated selling price in the ordinary course of business, less          銷售所需成本包括銷售直接應佔的增量成本及本集團為進
estimated costs necessary to make the sale of the respective inventories. Costs        行銷售而必須產生的非增量成本（包括市場推廣、銷售及分
necessary to make the sale include incremental costs directly attributable to the      銷過程中產生的成本）。
sale and non-incremental costs which the Group must incur to make the sale,
including costs to be incurred in marketing, selling and distribution.

pay the exercise price, if any, upon satisfaction of terms and conditions set out in   行使受限制股份，倘相關授出函件載列的條款及條件獲履
the relevant grant letter when they decide to exercise the RSUs. The participants      行，參與者則須支付行使價（如有）。有關參與者僅可於支

31. BUSINESS COMBINATION                                                               31. 業務合併
On 28 November 2024, the Group acquired the entire interest in OōEli, a company        於二零二四年十一月二十八日，本集團收購目里（一間最終
ultimately controlled by the controlling shareholders of the Group. OōEli is           由本集團控股股東控制的公司）的全部權益。目里主要經營
principally engaged in the operation of brands named “B1OCK”, a buyer-driven           名為「B1OCK」的品牌，B1OCK是一個融合當代藝術與生活
department store brand that blends contemporary art with lifestyle aesthetics.         美學的買手制百貨品牌。

The consideration is RMB1,672,000 in cash, which was determined between the            代價為現金人民幣1,672,000元，乃參考獨立估值師編製的
parties based on normal commercial terms with reference to a valuation report          估值報告，經訂約方按正常商業條款釐定。代價已支付，
prepared by an independent valuer. The consideration was paid and the acquisition      及收購事項已於二零二四年十一月完成。
was completed in November 2024.

The acquisition was considered as a business combination under common control          由於本集團及目里於收購事項前後均由同一控股股東最終
as the Group and OōEli are both ultimately controlled by the same controlling          控制且控制權並非暫時性，故收購事項被視為共同控制下
shareholders before and after the acquisition and the control is not transitory. The   的業務合併。收購目里乃根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港
acquisition of OōEli was accounted for using merger accounting in accordance with      會計指引第5號「共同控制合併之合併會計處理」
                                                                                                            （「會計指引
Hong Kong Accounting Guideline 5 “Merger Accounting for Common Control                 第5號」）採用合併會計處理入賬。
Combinations” (“AG 5”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public
Accountants.

36. SUMMARY OF OTHER ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                36. 其他會計政策概要
36.1 Basis of consolidation                                                             36.1 綜合基準

The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial statements of the       綜合財務報表納入本公司以及本公司及其附屬公司所控制
Company and entities controlled by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control is         實體的財務報表。當本公司符合下列條件時，即取得控制
achieved when the Company:                                                              權：

•     has power over the investee;                                                      •     可對投資對象行使權力；

•     is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its involvement with the      •     因參與投資對象業務而可獲得或有權獲得可變回報；
      investee; and                                                                           及

•     has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                           •     有能力藉行使其權力而影響該等回報。

The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts and                倘有事實或情況顯示上述三項控制因素中，有一項或以上
circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more of the three               出現變數，本集團會重新評估其是否控制投資對象。
elements of control listed above.

Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains control over the            本集團於獲得附屬公司控制權時將附屬公司綜合入賬，並
subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses control of the subsidiary. Specifically,     於失去附屬公司控制權時終止入賬。具體而言，年內購入
income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are         或出售附屬公司的收入及開支，乃自本集團獲得附屬公司
included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive        控制權當日起至本集團失去該控制權當日止，計入綜合損
income from the date the Group gains control until the date when the Group              益及其他全面收益表內。
ceases to control the subsidiary.

36.2 Business combinations or asset acquisitions                                           36.2 業務合併或資產收購

36.2.1 Optional concentration test                                                         36.2.1 可選集中性測試

The Group can elect to apply an optional concentration test, on a transaction-by-          本集團可按個別交易基準選擇應用可選集中性測試，允許
transaction basis, that permits a simplified assessment of whether an acquired set         對所收購的一組活動及資產是否為一項業務進行簡化評估。
of activities and assets is not a business. The concentration test is met if               倘所收購的總資產的絕大部分公允價值均集中於單個可識
substantially all of the fair value of the gross assets acquired is concentrated in a      別資產或一組類似的可識別資產中，則符合集中性測試。
single identifiable asset or group of similar identifiable assets. The gross assets        經評估的總資產不包括現金及現金等價物、遞延稅項資產
under assessment exclude cash and cash equivalents, deferred tax assets, and               及遞延稅項負債的影響所產生的商譽。倘符合集中性測試，
goodwill resulting from the effects of deferred tax liabilities. If the concentration      則該組活動及資產被釐定為並非業務而毋須進行進一步評
test is met, the set of activities and assets is determined not to be a business and       估。
no further assessment is needed.

36.2.2 Asset acquisitions                                                                  36.2.2 資產收購

When the Group acquires a group of assets and liabilities that do not constitute a         當本集團收購一組不構成業務的資產與負債，本集團先按
business, the Group identifies and recognises the individual identifiable assets           該等資產及負債各自的公允價值將購買價分配至金融資產╱
acquired and liabilities assumed by allocating the purchase price first to financial       金融負債，隨後按於購買日期各自的相對公允價值將購買
assets/financial liabilities at the respective fair values, the remaining balance of the   價餘額分配至其他可識別資產及負債，藉此識別並確認所
purchase price is then allocated to the other identifiable assets and liabilities on       收購的個別可識別資產及所承擔的負債。該項交易並無產
the basis of their relative fair values at the date of purchase. Such a transaction        生商譽或議價收購利得。
does not give rise to goodwill or bargain purchase gain.

36.2.3 Business combinations                                                             36.2.3 業務合併

A business is an integrated set of activities and assets which includes an input and     業務指一整套活動及資產，其中包括一項資源投入及一項
a substantive process that together significantly contribute to the ability to create    實質過程，兩者共同對創造產出的能力具有重大貢獻。倘
outputs. The acquired processes are considered substantive if they are critical to       收購過程對持續產出的能力至關重要（包括具備執行相關
the ability to continue producing outputs, including an organised workforce with the     過程所需技能、知識或經驗的組織化勞動力），或對持續產
necessary skills, knowledge, or experience to perform the related processes or           出的能力有重大貢獻，且屬獨特或稀缺，或在無重大成本、
they significantly contribute to the ability to continue producing outputs and are       努力或持續產出能力出現延遲的情況下不可取代，則收購
considered unique or scarce or cannot be replaced without significant cost, effort,      過程視為具實質性。
or delay in the ability to continue producing outputs.

Acquisitions of businesses, other than business combination under common control         除共同控制下業務合併外，其他業務收購採用收購法進行
are accounted for using the acquisition method. The consideration transferred in a       會計處理。業務合併轉讓代價按公允價值計量，公允價值
business combination is measured at fair value, which is calculated as the sum of        等於本集團轉讓資產、本集團對被收購方原擁有人承擔的
the acquisition-date fair values of the assets transferred by the Group, liabilities     負債，以及本集團為取得被收購方控制權而發行權益工具
incurred by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the equity                於收購日期公允價值的合計。收購相關成本一般於發生時
interests issued by the Group in exchange for control of the acquiree.                   於損益確認。
Acquisition-related costs are generally recognised in profit or loss as incurred.

When the consideration transferred by the Group in a business combination                若本集團於業務合併中轉讓的代價包括或然代價安排，該
includes a contingent consideration arrangement, the contingent consideration is         或然代價按收購日期公允價值計量，並計入業務合併的轉
measured at its acquisition-date fair value and included as part of the                  讓代價。符合計量期調整條件的或然代價公允價值變動作
consideration transferred in a business combination. Changes in the fair value of        追溯調整。計量期調整指在「計量期」
                                                                                                         （自收購日期起不超過
the contingent consideration that qualify as measurement period adjustments are          一年）取得與收購日期已存在事實及情況相關的額外資訊
adjusted retrospectively. Measurement period adjustments are adjustments that            而產生的調整。
arise from additional information obtained during the “measurement period” (which
cannot exceed one year from the acquisition date) about facts and circumstances
that existed at the acquisition date.

The subsequent accounting for the contingent consideration that do not qualify as        不符合計量期調整條件的或然代價，後續會計處理取決於
measurement period adjustments depends on how the contingent consideration is            該或然代價的分類。分類為權益的或然代價無需在後續報
classified. Contingent consideration that is classified as equity is not remeasured at   告日期重新計量，其後續結算於權益入賬。分類為資產或
subsequent reporting dates and its subsequent settlement is accounted for within         負債的或然代價，須於後續報告日期重新按公允價值計量，
equity. Contingent consideration that is classified as an asset or a liability is        相應利得或虧損於損益確認。
remeasured to fair value at subsequent reporting dates, with the corresponding
gain or loss being recognised in profit or loss.

36.3 Investments in associates                                                          36.3 於聯營公司的投資

An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant influence. Significant   聯營公司為本集團對其有重大影響力的實體。重大影響力
influence is the power to participate in the financial and operating policy decisions   指參與被投資對象的財務及營運政策決定的權力，但對該
of the investee but is not control or joint control over those policies.                等政策並無控制或共同控制權。

The results and assets and liabilities of associates are incorporated in these          聯營公司的業績、資產及負債採用權益會計法計入該等綜
consolidated financial statements using the equity method of accounting. Under the      合財務報表。根據權益法，於聯營公司的投資初步於綜合
equity method, an investment in an associate is initially recognised in the             財務狀況表按成本確認，其後就確認本集團應佔該聯營公
consolidated statement of financial position at cost and adjusted thereafter to         司的損益及其他全面收益作出調整。用作權益會計用途的
recognise the Group’s share of the profit or loss and other comprehensive income        聯營公司及合營企業的財務報表乃採用與本集團在類似情
of the associate. The financial statements of associates and joint ventures used for    況下處理同類交易及事項時所採用的統一會計政策編製。
equity accounting purposes are prepared using uniform accounting policies as            當本集團應佔聯營公司的虧損超過本集團於該聯營公司的
those of the Group for like transactions and events in similar circumstances. When      權益（包括任何實質上構成本集團於聯營公司投資淨額一
the Group’s share of losses of an associate exceeds the Group’s interest in that        部分的長期權益）時，本集團終止確認其應佔的進一步虧
associate (which includes any long-term interests that, in substance, form part of      損。僅於本集團已產生法定或推定責任或代表聯營公司付
the Group’s net investment in the associate), the Group discontinues recognising its    款時，方就額外虧損作出撥備及確認負債。
share of further losses. Additional losses are provided for, and a liability is
recognised only to the extent that the Group has incurred legal or constructive

(a)   The Group as a lessor                                                              (a)   本集團作為出租人

Classification and measurement of leases                                                 租賃的分類及計量

Leases for which the Group is a lessor are classified as finance or operating            本集團作為出租人的租賃分類為融資租賃或經營租賃。當
leases. Whenever the terms of the lease transfer substantially all the risks and         租賃的條款實質上將與相關資產所有權相關的所有風險及
rewards incidental to ownership of an underlying asset to the lessee, the contract       報酬轉讓給承租人時，該項合約被歸類為融資租賃。所有
is classified as a finance lease. All other leases are classified as operating leases.   其他租賃則歸類為經營租賃。

allocated to accounting periods so as to reflect a constant periodic rate of return      關租賃的未償還投資淨額的固定定期收益率。
on the Group’s net investment outstanding in respect of the leases.

under fair value model.

When a contract includes both leases and non-lease components, the Group                 當合約同時包括租賃及非租賃部分時，本集團應用香港財
applies HKFRS 15 to allocate consideration in a contract to lease and non-lease          務報告準則第15號將合約中的代價分配至租賃及非租賃部
components. Non-lease components are separated from lease component on the               分。非租賃部分因其相對獨立的售價而有別於租賃部分。
basis of their relative stand-alone selling prices.

Refundable rental deposits                                                               可退還租賃按金

Refundable rental deposits received are accounted for under HKFRS 9 and initially        已收的可退還租賃按金乃根據香港財務報告準則第9號入
measured at fair value. Adjustments to fair value at initial recognition are             賬，且初步按公允價值計量。於初始確認時對公允價值所
considered as additional lease payments from lessees.                                    作調整被視為承租人的額外租賃付款。

grants are intended to compensate.

expenses or losses already incurred or for the purpose of giving immediate            助，或為向本集團提供即時財務支持而收取且無未來相關

or loss in the period in which they become receivable. Such grants are presented      認。該等補助在「其他收益及利得淨額」項下列示。

purposes of assessing impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest levels for            配減值虧損時，應首先將減值虧損用於沖減任何商譽（如適
which there are separately identifiable cash flows (cash-generating units). In           用）的賬面值，然後根據該現金產生單位或現金產生單位組

of cash-generating units. The carrying amount of an asset is not reduced below the       的減值虧損金額，將按比例分配至該現金產生單位或現金
highest of its fair value less costs of disposal (if measurable), its value in use (if   產生單位組別內的其他資產。除商譽外，已蒙受減值的非

or loss.

36.12.4 Impairment                                                                    36.12.4 減值

The Group assesses on a forward-looking basis the expected credit losses              本集團按前瞻性原則，對按攤銷成本列賬的債務工具相關
associated with its debt instruments carried at amortised cost. The impairment        的預期信貸虧損進行評估。所採用的減值方法取決於信貸
methodology applied depends on whether there has been a significant increase in       風險是否顯著增加。
credit risk.

(i)   Significant increase in credit risk                                             (i)   信貸風險顯著增加

In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly since initial        為評估信貸風險在初始確認後有否顯著增加，本集團會將
recognition, the Group compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial      金融工具在報告日期發生的違約風險與金融工具在初始確
instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a default occurring on the       認日期發生的違約風險相比較。本集團進行評估時，會考
financial instrument as at the date of initial recognition. In making this            慮合理並有充分支持的定量及定性資料（包括過往經驗及
assessment, the Group considers both quantitative and qualitative information that    毋須耗費不必要費用或資源即可取得的前瞻性信息）。所考
is reasonable and supportable, including historical experience and forward-looking    慮的前瞻性資料包括獲取自經濟專家報告、金融分析師及
information that is available without undue cost or effort. Forward-looking           政府機構的本集團債務人經營所在行業的未來前景，亦會
information considered includes the future prospects of the industries in which the   參考多個外部來源中與本集團核心業務相關的實際及預測
Group’s debtors operate, obtained from economic expert reports, financial analysts    經濟資料。
and governmental bodies, as well as consideration of various external sources of
actual and forecast economic information that relate to the Group’s core
operations.

(i)    Significant increase in credit risk (Continued)                                     (i)    信貸風險顯著增加（續）

In particular, the following information is taken into account when assessing              具體而言，在評估信貸風險有否顯著增加時，會考慮以下
whether credit risk has increased significantly:                                           資料：

•      an actual or expected significant deterioration in the financial instrument’s       •      金融工具的外部（如有）或內部信用評級實際上或預
       external (if available) or internal credit rating;                                         期顯著惡化；

•      significant deterioration in external market indicators of credit risk, e.g. a      •      信貸風險的外部市場指標大幅惡化，例如信貸息差
       significant increase in the credit spread, the credit default swap prices for              大幅增加、債務人的信貸違約掉期價大幅上升；
       the debtor;

•      existing or forecast adverse changes in business, financial or economic             •      預期將會導致債務人履行其償債義務的能力發生顯
       conditions that are expected to cause a significant decrease in the debtor’s               著減少的業務、財務或經濟狀況出現現有或預測不
       ability to meet its debt obligations;                                                      利變化；

•      an actual or expected significant deterioration in the operating results of the     •      債務人經營業績實際或預期顯著惡化；
       debtor;

•      an actual or expected significant adverse change in the regulatory, economic,       •      導致債務人履行其償債義務的能力發生顯著減少的
       or technological environment of the debtor that results in a significant                   監管、經濟或債務人技術環境出現實際或預測顯著
       decrease in the debtor’s ability to meet its debt obligations.                             不利變化。

When contractual payments are more than 30 days past due, the Group may                    如合約付款逾期超過30日，本集團可能根據過往經驗及債
consider that the credit risk has not increased significantly based on past                務人的良好還款紀錄，認為信貸風險並未顯著增加。
experience and satisfactory settlement history from the debtors.

(ii)   Definition of default                                                               (ii)   違約的定義

For internal credit risk management, the Group considers an event of default               就內部信貸風險管理而言，本集團認為當內部生成或從外
occurs when information developed internally or obtained from external sources             部來源所得資料顯示，債務人不大可能向其債權人（包括本
indicates that the debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors, including the Group, in full   集團）支付全數款項（並無計及本集團所持的任何抵押品）
(without taking into account any collaterals held by the Group).                           時，則出現違約事件。

When a financial asset is more than 90 days past due, it may not be considered as          如金融資產逾期超過90日，鑒於債務人的過往及預期後續
in default by the Group considering the historical and expected subsequent                 還款情況，本集團可能不會將此視為違約。
repayment from the debtors.

(iii)   Credit-impaired financial assets                                                   (iii)   信貸減值金融資產

A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events that have a                   如發生一項或多項事件會對金融資產估計未來現金流量造
detrimental impact on the estimated future cash flows of that financial asset have         成不利的影響，則有關金融資產會被評定為出現信貸減值。
occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired includes observable           金融資產出現信貸減值的證據包括有關下列事件的可觀察
data about the following events:                                                           數據：

(a)     significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the borrower;                    (a)     發行人或借款人面臨重大財務困難；

(b)     a breach of contract, such as a default or past due event;                         (b)     違反合約，例如違約或逾期事件；

(c)     the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or contractual reasons relating        (c)     借款人的貸款人因有關借款人關於經濟或合約理由
        to the borrower’s financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower a                   上的財務困難，而向借款人批出該貸款人在其他情
        concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise consider; or                          況下不會考慮的特許權；或

(d)     it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter bankruptcy or other           (d)     借款人將可能陷入破產或其他財務重組。
        financial reorganization.

For trade receivables, the Group applies the simplified approach permitted by              就應收賬款而言，本集團採用香港財務報告準則第9號允許
HKFRS 9, which requires expected lifetime losses to be recognised from initial             的簡化方法，其中要求全期預期虧損須自初始確認應收款
recognition of the receivables, see Note 17 for further details. The Group has             項時確認，有關更多詳情請參閱附註17。本集團已根據其
established a provision matrix that is based on its historical credit loss experience,     歷史信貸虧損訂立撥備矩陣，且就債務人及經濟環境的特
adjusted for forward-looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic              定前瞻性因素作出調整。
environment.

For deposits and other receivables, management considers that its credit risk has          就按金及其他應收款項而言，管理層認為，經參考對手方
not increased significantly since initial recognition with reference to the                歷史違約率及當前財務狀況後，其信貸風險自初始確認後
counterparty historical default rate and current financial position. The impairment        並無大幅增加。按12個月預期信貸虧損釐定的減值撥備接
provision is determined based on the 12-month expected credit losses, which is             近零。為評估信貸風險是否大幅增加，本集團對比資產於
close to zero. To assess whether there is a significant increase in credit risk, the       報告日期的違約風險，違約風險透過計入可得、合理及可
Group compares the risk of default occurring on the asset as at the reporting date         靠的前瞻性資料於初始確認日期計量。
with the risk of default as at the date of initial recognition by considering available,
reasonable and supportive forwarding-looking information.

the Group are recognised at the proceeds received, net of direct issue costs.                 除直接發行成本後確認。

payment of distribution and redemption of principal amount indefinitely are                   分配及贖回本金）被分類為權益工具。
classified as equity instruments.

sale, issue or cancellation of the Company’s own equity instruments.                          不會於損益確認。

36.13.4 Offsetting financial instruments                                                 36.13.4 金融工具的抵銷

Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount reported in the           當有法定可強制執行權力可抵銷已確認金額，並有意圖按
balance sheet when there is a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised         其淨額基準結算或同時變現資產和結算負債時，有關金融
amounts and there is an intention to settle on a net basis or realise the asset and      資產與負債可互相抵銷，並於資產負債表報告其淨額。法
settle the liability simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not be           定可執行權利必須不得依賴未來事件而定，而在一般業務
contingent on future events and must be enforceable in the normal course of              過程中以及倘公司或對手方一旦出現違約、無償債能力或
business and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the company or         破產時，這也必須具有約束力。
the counterparty.

36.14 Trade and other receivables                                                        36.14 應收賬款及其他應收款項

Trade receivables are amounts due from customers for merchandise sold in the             應收賬款為在日常業務過程中就售出貨品應收客戶的款項。
ordinary course of business. If collection of trade and other receivables is expected    如應收賬款及其他應收款項預期在一年或以內（如在業務
in one year or less (or in the normal operating cycle of the business if longer), they   的正常營運週期，則可較長時間）收回，分類為流動資產，
are classified as current assets. If not, they are presented as non-current assets.      否則分類為非流動資產。

Trade and other receivables are recognised initially at fair value and subsequently      應收賬款及其他應收款項按公允價值初始確認，其後以實
measured at amortised cost using the effective interest method, less provision for       際利率法按攤銷成本扣除減值撥備計量。有關本集團減值
impairment. See Note 36.12.4 for description of the Group’s impairment policies.         政策的闡述請參閱附註36.12.4。

36.15 Cash and cash equivalents                                                          36.15 現金及現金等價物

For the purpose of presentation in the statement of cash flows, cash and cash            就呈列現金流量表而言，現金及現金等價物包括手頭現金、
equivalents includes cash on hand, deposits held at call with financial institutions,    金融機構的活期存款、可隨時轉換為已知數額現金且價值
other short-term, highly liquid investments with original maturities of three months     變動風險甚微的原定到期日為三個月或以下的其他高流動
or less that are readily convertible to known amounts of cash and which are              性短期投資以及銀行透支。
subject to an insignificant risk of changes in value, and bank overdrafts.

36.16 Provisions                                                                         36.16 撥備

Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present obligation (legal or              倘本集團因過往事件而須承擔現時責任（法律或推定），而
constructive) as a result of a past event, it is probable that the Group will be         本集團可能須履行該責任且該責任的金額能可靠估計，則
required to settle that obligation, and a reliable estimate can be made of the           會確認撥備。
amount of the obligation.

36.16 Provisions (Continued)                                                                36.16 撥備（續）

The amount recognised as a provision is the best estimate of the consideration              經計及有關責任之風險及不確定因素後，確認為撥備的金
required to settle the present obligation at the end of the reporting period, taking        額為於報告期末就履行現時責任所需代價作出的最佳估計。
into account the risks and uncertainties surrounding the obligation. When a                 當使用估計用以履行現時責任的現金流量計量撥備時，其
provision is measured using the cash flows estimated to settle the present                  賬面值為該等現金流量的現值（倘貨幣時間價值的影響屬
obligation, its carrying amount is the present value of those cash flows (when the          重大）。
effect of the time value of money is material).

36.17 Borrowings                                                                            36.17 借款

Borrowings are initially recognised at fair value, net of transaction costs incurred.       借款按公允價值（扣除所產生的交易成本）初始確認。借款
Borrowings are subsequently measured at amortised cost. Any difference between              其後按攤銷成本計量。所得款項（扣除交易成本）與贖回款
the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the redemption amount is recognised in          項之間的任何差額以實際利率法於借款期間內於損益確認。
profit or loss over the period of the borrowings using the effective interest method.       倘貸款融資很有可能部分或全部被提取，則就設立貸款融
Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as transaction             資時支付的費用確認為貸款交易成本。在此情況下，費用
costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some or all of the facility will   遞延至提取發生為止。倘無任何證據顯示該貸款很有可能
be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred until the draw-down occurs. To the         部分或全部提取，則該費用撥充資本作為流動資金服務的
extent there is no evidence that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be    預付款，並於有關融資期間攤銷。
drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a prepayment for liquidity services and
amortised over the period of the facility to which it relates.

Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless, at the end of the reporting        除非本集團於報告期末有權將負債結算日期押後至報告期
period, the Group has a right to defer settlement of the liability for at least 12          間後至少12個月，否則借款將被分類為流動負債。
months after the reporting period.

36.18 Research and development expenditure                                                  36.18 研發開支

which it is incurred.

36.19 Current and deferred income tax                                                       36.19 當期及遞延所得稅

The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax payable on the current           期內所得稅開支或抵免指根據各司法管轄權區的適用所得
period’s taxable income based on the applicable income tax rate for each                    稅率按當期應課稅收入支付的稅項，而有關所得稅率經暫
jurisdiction adjusted by changes in deferred tax assets and liabilities attributable to     時性差額及未動用稅項虧損所致的遞延稅項資產及負債變
temporary differences and to unused tax losses.                                             動調整。

(a)    Current income tax                                                                   (a)   當期所得稅

The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the tax laws enacted or         當期所得稅開支乃根據本公司的附屬公司及聯營公司營運
substantively enacted at the balance sheet date in the countries where the                  及產生應課稅收入所在國家按於資產負債表日已頒佈或實
Company’s subsidiaries and associates operate and generate taxable income.                  質上已頒佈的稅法計算。管理層就適用稅務法例詮釋所規
Management periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect to            限的情況定期評估報稅表的狀況，並在適用情況下按預期
situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation. It              須向稅務機關支付的稅款作出撥備。
establishes provisions where appropriate on the basis of amounts expected to be
paid to the tax authorities.

Inside basis differences                                                                 內在差異

Deferred income tax is recognised, using the liability method, on temporary              遞延所得稅以負債法就資產及負債的稅基與綜合財務報表
differences arising between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their            所示資產及負債賬面值的暫時性差額予以確認。然而，若
carrying amounts in the consolidated financial statements. However, deferred tax         遞延稅項負債來自對商譽的初始確認，則不予確認；以及
liabilities are not recognised if they arise from the initial recognition of goodwill,   若遞延所得稅來自在交易（不包括業務合併）中對資產或負
the deferred income tax is not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of    債的初始確認，而在交易時不影響會計處理或應課稅損益，
an asset or liability in a transaction other than a business combination that at the     則不作列賬。遞延所得稅按在資產負債表日前已頒佈或實
time of the transaction affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss.           質上已頒佈且預期在有關遞延所得稅資產變現或遞延所得
Deferred income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been              稅負債結算時將應用的稅率（及法例）釐定。
enacted or substantively enacted by the balance sheet date and are expected to

Deferred tax assets are recognised only if it is probable that future taxable            遞延稅項資產僅於未來應課稅金額將有可能用於動用該等
amounts will be available to utilise those temporary differences and losses.             暫時性差額及虧損時確認。

Outside basis differences                                                                外在差異

Deferred income tax liabilities are provided on taxable temporary differences            遞延所得稅負債按於附屬公司的投資所產生的應課稅暫時
arising from investments in subsidiaries, except for deferred income tax liability       性差額作撥備，惟倘就遞延所得稅負債而言，撥回暫時性
where the timing of the reversal of the temporary difference is controlled by the        差額的時間由本集團控制，而在可見將來不大可能撥回暫
Group and it is probable that the temporary difference will not reverse in the           時性差額則除外。
foreseeable future.

Deferred income tax assets are recognised on deductible temporary differences            遞延所得稅資產僅會於暫時性差額有可能於未來撥回及有
arising from investments in subsidiaries, only to the extent that it is probable the     充足應課稅利潤可用以動用暫時性差額時就附屬公司投資
temporary difference will reverse in the future and there is sufficient taxable profit   產生的可扣稅暫時性差額確認。
available against which the temporary difference can be utilised.

(c)   Offsetting                                                                         (c)   抵銷

Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset where there is a legally enforceable      當有法定可強制執行權利將當期稅項資產與負債抵銷，而
right to offset current tax assets and liabilities and where the deferred tax            遞延稅項結餘與同一稅務機構相關時，則可將遞延稅項資
balances relate to the same taxation authority. Current tax assets and tax liabilities   產與負債抵銷。當實體有法定可強制執行權利抵銷且有意
are offset where the entity has a legally enforceable right to offset and intends        按淨額基準結算或同時變現資產及清償負債時，則當期稅
either to settle on a net basis, or to realise the asset and settle the liability        項資產與稅項負債互相抵銷。
simultaneously.
